    class PortalOpenApiClient(builtins.object)
     |  PortalOpenApiClient(config: 'PortalOpenApiConfig', *, http_client: 'httpx.AsyncClient | None' = None) -> 'None'
     |
     |  Server-side M2M client for iam-portal ``/open/v1/**``.
     |
     |  The client requests ``client_credentials`` machine tokens from iam-server
     |  with explicit ``portal:openapi:read`` or ``portal:openapi:write`` scope,
     |  caches read/write tokens separately, and sends only bearer auth to Portal.
     |
     |  Methods defined here:

     |      cased characters have lower case.
     |
     |  translate(self, table, /)

     |        table

     |          Unicode ordinals, strings, or None.
     |
     |      The table must implement lookup/indexing via __getitem__, for instance a
     |      dictionary or list.  If this operation raises LookupError, the character is
     |      left untouched.  Characters mapped to None are deleted.

     |      If there is only one argument, it must be a dictionary mapping Unicode
     |      ordinals (integers) or characters to Unicode ordinals, strings or None.
     |      Character keys will be then converted to ordinals.
     |      If there are two arguments, they must be strings of equal length, and

     |          Translation table, which must be a mapping of Unicode ordinals to
     |          Unicode ordinals, strings, or None.
     |
     |      The table must implement lookup/indexing via __getitem__, for instance a
     |      dictionary or list.  If this operation raises LookupError, the character is
     |      left untouched.  Characters mapped to None are deleted.
     |
     |  upper(self, /)
     |      Return a copy of the string converted to uppercase.
     |
     |  zfill(self, width, /)
     |      Pad a numeric string with zeros on the left, to fill a field of the given width.
     |
     |      The string is never truncated.
     |
     |  ----------------------------------------------------------------------
     |  Static methods inherited from builtins.str:
     |
     |  maketrans(...)
     |      Return a translation table usable for str.translate().
     |
     |      If there is only one argument, it must be a dictionary mapping Unicode
     |      ordinals (integers) or characters to Unicode ordinals, strings or None.
     |      Character keys will be then converted to ordinals.
     |      If there are two arguments, they must be strings of equal length, and
     |      in the resulting dictionary, each character in x will be mapped to the
     |      character at the same position in y. If there is a third argument, it
     |      must be a string, whose characters will be mapped to None in the result.

        Return whether a permission set contains ``resource:action``.

    matches_permission(target: 'PermissionKey', granted: 'str') -> 'bool'
        Return whether ``granted`` satisfies ``target``.

        Matching is case-sensitive and aligned with Java/Go/JS SDK behavior:
        exact, resource action wildcard, and full wildcard. ``*:action`` and
        malformed/non-canonical granted strings never match.

    parse_permission(value: 'str') -> 'PermissionKey | None'

    class MemorySessionStore(builtins.object)
     |  MemorySessionStore(*, max_sessions: 'int' = 10000, max_prelogins: 'int' = 1000, max_record_bytes: 'int' = 65536, cleanup_interval: 'float' = 60, clock: 'Callable[[], float]' = <built-in function time>)
     |
     |  Single-process, thread/coroutine-safe, volatile store; no implicit fallback.
     |
     |  Each operation copies and bounds a record while holding an instance lock.
     |  It performs no blocking IO in its async methods. Close joins the cleanup
     |  thread and clears credentials; closed operations report CLOSED, not ABSENT.

    class ServerSession(builtins.object)
     |  ServerSession(config: 'SessionConfig', store: 'AsyncSessionStore', *, http_client: 'httpx.AsyncClient | None' = None, clock: 'Callable[[], float]' = <built-in function time>)
